Complete\n159\t\n160\t**├É┬í├É┬É├É┼ô├É┼╛ ├É┬É├É┼í├É┼╛ ├ÉΓÇÖ├É┬í├É╦£├É┬º├É┼í├É╦£ ├É┼í├É┬á├É╦£├É┬ó├ÉΓÇó├É┬á├É╦£├É╦£ ├É┬í├É┬É ├ó┼ôΓÇª:**\n161\t\n162\t1. **Update C:/Users/kaloyan.georgiev/Projects/ralph/tasks.json:**\n163\t   ```json\n164\t   // Change for completed task:\n165\t   \"passes\": false ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ \"passes\": true\n166\t   ```\n167\t\n168\t2. **Log code\n257\t\n258\t**The Ralph loop will spawn a NEW agent with clean context for the next task.**\n259\t\n260\t## Code Intelligence: GitNexus\n261\t\n262\t├óΓÇ║ΓÇ¥ **├É┬¥├É╦£├É┼í├É┼╛├ÉΓÇ£├É┬É ├É┬╜├É┬╡   - `feat(compositions): {exact task.description from tasks.json}`\n   - Example: `feat(compositions): Create Dashboard List Page with compositions table`\n   \n   ---\n   ```\n\n3. **Git commit:**\n   \n   **IMPORTANT:** Use the exact task `description` from tasks.json as commit message!\n   \n   ```bash\n   git add .\n   git commit -m \"feat(compositions): {exact task.description from tasks.json}\"\n   ```\n   \n   **Example:** For Task #11 with description \"Create Dashboard List Page with compositions table\":\n   ```bash\n   git commit -m \"feat(compositions): Create Dashboard List Page with compositions table\"\n   ```\n   \n   **DO NOT:**\n   - git init\n   - git push\n   - change remotes\n   - make up your own commit message (use exact description!)\n\n### Step 5: Report Status AND STOP\n\n**Output exactly this, then STOP ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ do NOT continue with another task:**\n\n```xml\n<task-complete>\n  <task-id>{id}</task-id>\n  <tests>PASSED</tests>\n  <visual>MATCHED</visual>\n  <committed>YES</committed>\n</task-complete>\n```\n\n**Then check: are there more tasks with passes: false?**\n\nIf YES ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ output this and **STOP IMMEDIATELY** (next task = next iteration = new agent):\n```xml\n<status>CONTINUE</status>\n<next-task>{next_task_id}</next-task>\n```\n\nIf NO (all tasks done) ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ output:\n```xml\n<promise>COMPLETE</promise>\n<total-tasks>{count}</total-tasks>\n<all-passed>true</all-passed>\n```\n\n### ├░┼╕ΓÇ║ΓÇÿ AFTER Step 5: YOUR ITERATION IS OVER\n\n**Do NOT:**\n- Start working on the next task\n- Read the next task's steps\n- \"Prepare\" anything for the next iteration\n- Continue writing code\n\n**The Ralph loop will spawn a NEW agent with clean context for the UI flow ├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├É┬╡ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬▓├É┬░ ├æ┬ü unit. ├É┬É├É┬║├É┬╛ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├æ┬ü├É┬║├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╖├É┬░├æ┬ü├æ┬Å├É┬│├É┬░ ├É┬╜├æ┬Å├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╗├É┬║├É┬╛ ├É┬╜├É┬╕├É┬▓├É┬░ ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├É┬┐├É┬╕├æ╦å├É┬╡├æ╦å ├É┬╜├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æ┬ü├æ┬Å├É┬║├É┬╛, ├É┬╜├É┬╛ ├É┬¥├ÉΓÇó ├É┬┤├æ╞Æ├É┬▒├É┬╗├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░├æ╦å ├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬╕├É┬╜ ├É┬╕ ├æ┬ü├æ┼á├æΓÇ░ assertion.\n395\t\n396\t**Anti-pattern (├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╛├É┬┐├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╜├É┬░ team-├É┬░):** `if (await el.isVisible()) { await expect(el).toBeVisible(); }` ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╝├É┬╕├É┬╜├É┬░├É┬▓├É┬░ ├É┬▓├É┬╕├É┬╜├É┬░├É┬│├É┬╕. ├É┬É├É┬║├É┬╛ ├É┬╡├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬╝├É┬╡├É┬╜├æΓÇÜ├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ ├æΓÇÜ├æΓé¼├æ┬Å├É┬▒├É┬▓├É┬░ ├É┬┤├É┬░ ├É┬╡ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├É┬╝ ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ assert-├É┬▓├É┬░├É┬╣ ├É┬│├É┬╛ ├É┬┤├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬║├æΓÇÜ├É┬╜├É┬╛, ├É┬▒├É┬╡├É┬╖ `if` guard. Failing test, ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╣├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├É┬▓├É┬╕├É┬╜├É┬░├É┬│├É┬╕ passes, ├É┬╡ ├É┬┐├É┬╛-├É┬╗├É┬╛├æ╦å ├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╜├É┬╕├É┬║├É┬░├É┬║├æ┼á├É┬▓ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ.\n397\t\n398\t### ├ó┼í┬í Selective E2E (inner loop optimization)\n399\t\n400\t**├É┼╕├æΓé¼├É┬╕├É┬╜├æΓÇá├É┬╕├É┬┐:** ├É┬ª├É┬╡├É┬╗├É┬╕├æ┬Å├æΓÇÜ Playwright suite ├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├É┬║├æ┼á├É┬┐ (5-20 ├É┬╝├É┬╕├É┬╜). ├É┬¥├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æ┬ü├æ┬Å├É┬║├É┬░ ├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╗├æΓÇ₧ ├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æΓé¼├É┬░├æΓÇá├É┬╕├æ┬Å ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├É┬┐├æ╞Æ├æ┬ü├É┬║├É┬░├É┬╣ **├É┬í├É┬É├É┼ô├É┼╛** specs, ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├É┬╝├É┬╛├É┬│├É┬░├æΓÇÜ ├É┬┤├É┬░ ├É┬▒├æ┼á├É┬┤├É┬░├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╖├É┬░├æ┬ü├É┬╡├É┬│├É┬╜├É┬░├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕ ├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬▓├É┬╛├æ┬Å diff. webServer ├æΓÇ░├É┬╡ detect-├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├æ┼á├æΓÇ░├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬▓├æ╞Æ├É┬▓├É┬░├æΓÇ░├É┬╕├æ┬Å ├É┬╕ ├æΓÇ░├É┬╡ ├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬╛├É┬┐├æ╞Æ├æ┬ü├É┬╜├É┬╡ 30-60s startup ├É┬╖├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æ┬ü├É┬╡├É┬║├É┬╕ ├æ┬ü├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬▓├É┬░├æΓÇ░ E2E run. ├É┬í├É┬┐├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├æ┬Å├É┬▓├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬╝├É┬╡ ├âΓÇö N iterations.\n411\t\n412\t**Recipe (├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬│├É┬░├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ DONE phase ├É┬╕├É┬╖├É┬╕├æ┬ü├É┬║├É┬▓├É┬░ E2E run):**\n413\t\n414\t```powershell\n415\t# 1. ├É┼╕├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├É┬╣ ├æ┬ü├É┬║├æΓé¼├É┬╕├É┬┐├æΓÇÜ├É┬░ ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╕ specs ├æ┬ü├É┬░ ├É┬╜├æ╞Æ├É┬╢├É┬╜├É┬╕. ├É┼╕├É┬╛ default ├æ┬ü├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬▓├É┬╜├æ┬Å├É┬▓├É┬░ ├æ┬ü├æΓé¼├É┬╡├æΓÇ░├æ╞Æ origin/develop.\n416\t$selection = powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File C:/Users/kaloyan.georgiev/Projects/ralph/select-e2e.ps1\n417\t\n418\t# 2. ├É┬á├É┬╡├É┬░├É┬│├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╣ ├æ┬ü├É┬┐├É┬╛├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬┤ output-├É┬░:\n419\tif ($selection -eq 'FULL') {\n420\t    # Cross-cutting ├É┬┐├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╡ bumped (auth, routing, store, locales, shared/components, ├óΓé¼┬ª).\n421\t    # Path-mapping ├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├É┬╝├É┬╛├É┬╢├É┬╡ ├É┬┤├É┬░ ├æΓÇÜ├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬╜├æ┬ü├É┬╡ global state side-effects ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ ├É┬┐├æ┼á├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬╜ suite.\n422\t    npm run e2e\n423\t}\n424\telseif ($selection) {\n425\t    # 1+ feature ├É┬╝├É┬░p├É┬╜├É┬░├æΓÇÜ ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├æΓé¼├É┬│├É┬╡├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╜ run ├É┬╜├É┬░ ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╜├É┬║├æΓé¼├É┬╡├æΓÇÜ├É┬╜├É┬╕ spec ├æΓÇ₧├É┬░├É┬╣├É┬╗├É┬╛├É┬▓├É┬╡/├É┬┐├É┬░├É┬┐├É┬║├É┬╕.\n426\t    npm run e2e -- $selection.Split()\n427\t}\n428\telse {\n429\t    # Diff ├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├æ┼á├É┬┤├æ┼á├æΓé¼├É┬╢├É┬░ UI-relevant ├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬╛├É┬╝├É┬╡├É┬╜├É┬╕ (backend-only, docs, ralph/, ├æΓÇí├É┬╕├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ ├æΓÇ₧├É┬░├É┬╣├É┬╗├É┬╛├É┬▓├É┬╡).\n430\t    # Skip E2E. A TIME** - Never work on multiple tasks in parallel\n2. **ONE STEP AT A TIME** - Execute steps sequentially, verify after each\n3. **TESTS FIRST** (for TDD tasks) - Write failing test BEFORE implementation. **├ÉΓÇÖ├É┬╕├É┬╢ \"Test level selection\" ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├É┬╕├É┬╖├É┬▒├É┬╛├æΓé¼├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╜├É┬░ ├É┬╜├É┬╕├É┬▓├É┬╛ (unit / integration / E2E) ├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├É┬╡ ├É┬░├É┬▓├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛├É┬╝├É┬░├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕├æΓÇí├É┬╡├É┬╜; ├É┬╖├É┬░├É┬▓├É┬╕├æ┬ü├É┬╕ ├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ ├É┬║├É┬░├É┬║├É┬▓├É┬╛ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬▓├É┬░ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛├É┬╖├É┬╕ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├æ┬ü├É┬║.**\n4. **VISUAL FEEDBACK MANDATORY** (for designReference tasks) - Must screenshot and compare\n5. **ITERATE UNTIL PASS** - Don't mark complete until tests pass AND design matches\n\n### ├░┼╕┬º┬¬ Test level selection (RED Failing test, ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╣├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├É┬▓├É┬╕├É┬╜├É┬░├É┬│├É┬╕ passes, ├É┬╡ ├É┬┐├É┬╛-├É┬╗├É┬╛├æ╦å ├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╜├É┬╕├É┬║├É┬░├É┬║├æ┼á├É┬▓ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ.\n\n### ├ó┼í┬í Selective E2E (inner loop optimization)\n\n**├É┼╕├æΓé¼├É┬╕├É┬╜├æΓÇá├É┬╕├É┬┐:** ├É┬ª├É┬╡├É┬╗├É┬╕├æ┬Å├æΓÇÜ Playwright suite ├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├É┬║├æ┼á├É┬┐ (5-20 ├É┬╝├É┬╕├É┬╜). ├É┬¥├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æ┬ü├æ┬Å├É┬║├É┬░ ├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╗├æΓÇ₧ ├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æΓé¼├É┬░├æΓÇá├É┬╕├æ┬Å ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├É┬┐├æ╞Æ├æ┬ü├É┬║├É┬░├É┬╣ **├É┬í├É┬É├É┼ô├É┼╛** specs, ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├É┬╝├É┬╛├É┬│├É┬░├æΓÇÜ ├É┬┤├É┬░ ├É┬▒├æ┼á├É┬┤├É┬░├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╖├É┬░├æ┬ü├É┬╡├É┬│├É┬╜├É┬░├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕ ├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬▓├É┬╛├æ┬Å diff. CI/PR pipeline ├æΓÇ░├É┬╡ ├É┬┐├æ╞Æ├æ┬ü├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├É┬┐├æ┼á├É┬╗├É┬╜├É┬╕├æ┬Å suite ├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬╕ merge ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├æ┬ü├É┬╡├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬║├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕├É┬▓├É┬╜├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├É┬░├É┬╝├É┬╛ ├É┬╖├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ├æΓé¼├É┬╡├æ╦å├É┬╜├É┬╕├æ┬Å ├æΓÇá├É┬╕├É┬║├æ┼á├É┬╗.\n\n**├░┼╕┼╕┬ó Dev server ├É┬╖├É┬░ E2E ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ auto-managed ├æΓÇí├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬╖ Playwright `webServer`:** ├É┼╕├æΓé¼├É┬╛├É┬▓├É┬╡├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬╜├É┬╛ ├É┬▓ [`e2e/playwright.config.ts`](C:/Users/kaloyan.georgiev/Projects/Admin-App/e2e/playwright.config.ts) (├É┬╕├É┬╖├É┬┐├É┬╛├É┬╗├É┬╖├É┬▓├É┬░├É┬╜├É┬╕├æ┬Å├æΓÇÜ config ├É┬╖├É┬░ `npm run e2e`):\n- `webServer.command: 'npm run dev'` ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Playwright ├æ┬ü├É┬░├É┬╝ ├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├æΓé¼├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░ dev server-├É┬░\n- `webServer.url: 'http://localhost:3000'` (├É┬╜├É┬╡ 5173 ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ `npm run dev` ├É┬╡ ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╜├æΓÇ₧├É┬╕├É┬│├æ╞Æ├æΓé¼├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╜ ├É┬┤├É┬░ ├æ┬ü├É┬╗├æ╞Æ├æ╦å├É┬░ ├É┬╜├É┬░ 3000)\n- `webServer.reuseExistingServer: true` ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├É┬░├É┬║├É┬╛ ├É┬▓├É┬╡├æΓÇí├É┬╡ ├É┬╕├É┬╝├É┬░ running dev server, ├É┬│├É┬╛ reuse-├É┬▓├É┬░\n- `webServer.timeout: 120_000` ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ ├É┬╕├É┬╖├æΓÇí├É┬░├É┬║├É┬▓├É┬░ ├É┬┤├É┬╛ 120s ├É┬╖├É┬░ startup\n\n├óΓÇáΓÇÖ **├É┬á├É┬░├É┬╗├æΓÇ₧ ├É┬¥├ÉΓÇó ├æΓÇÜ├æΓé¼├æ┬Å├É┬▒├É┬▓├É┬░ ├æΓé¼├æ┼á├æΓÇí├É┬╜├É┬╛ ├É┬┤├É┬░ fork-├É┬▓├É┬░ `npm run dev`** ├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬╕ `npm run e2e` ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ Playwright ├æΓÇ░├É┬╡ ├É┬│├É┬╛ ├É┬╜├É┬░├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬▓├É┬╕. ├É┼╕├æ┼á├æΓé¼├É┬▓├É┬╕├æ┬Å├æΓÇÜ E2E run ├É┬▓ ├æ┬ü├É┬╡├æ┬ü├É┬╕├æ┬Å ├É┬┐├É┬╗├É┬░├æΓÇ░├É┬░ 30-60s startup; ├æ┬ü├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬▓├É┬░├æΓÇ░├É┬╕ run-├É┬╛├É┬▓├É┬╡ (├æ┬ü reuseExistingServer) ├æ┬ü├É┬░ ├É┬▒├æ┼á├æΓé¼├É┬╖├É┬╕.\n\n**Tip ├É┬╖├É┬░ ├É┬┤├æ┼á├É┬╗├É┬│├É┬░ ├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╗├æΓÇ₧ ├É┬╜├É┬╛├æΓÇ░├É┬╜├É┬░ ├æ┬ü├É┬╡├æ┬ü├É┬╕├æ┬Å:** ├É┬É├É┬║├É┬╛ ├É┬┐├æ╞Æ├æ┬ü├É┬║├É┬░├æ╦å ├É┬╝├É┬╜├É┬╛├É┬│├É┬╛ iterations ├æ┬ü E2E ├óΓé¼ΓÇ¥ fork `npm run dev` ├É┬▓├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬╜├æ┼á├É┬╢ ├É┬▓ background (Bash tool ├æ┬ü `run_in_background: true`) ├É┬▓ ├É┬╜├É┬░├æΓÇí├É┬░├É┬╗├É┬╛├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├É┬╜├É┬░ ├æ┬ü├É┬╡├æ┬ü├É┬╕├æ┬Å├æΓÇÜ├É┬░; webServer ├æΓÇ░├É┬╡ detect-├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├æ┼á├æΓÇ░├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬▓├æ╞Æ├É┬▓├É┬░├æΓÇ░├É┬╕├æ┬Å ├É┬╕ ├æΓÇ░├É┬╡ ├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬╛├É┬┐├æ╞Æ├æ┬ü├É┬╜├É┬╡ 30-60s startup ├É┬╖├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æ┬ü├É┬╡├É┬║├É┬╕ ├æ┬ü├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬┤├É┬▓├É┬░├æΓÇ░ E2E run. ├É┬í├É┬┐├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├æ┬Å├É┬▓├É┬░ ├É┬▓├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬╝├É┬╡ ├âΓÇö N iterations.\n\n**Recipe (├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬│├É┬░├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ DONE phase ├É┬╕├É┬╖├É┬╕├æ┬ü├É┬║├É┬▓├É┬░ E2E run):**\n\n```powershell\n# 1. ├É┼╕├É┬╕├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├É┬╣ ├æ┬ü├É┬║├æΓé¼├É┬╕├É┬┐├æΓÇÜ├É┬░ ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╕ specs ├æ┬ü├É┬░ ├É┬╜├æ╞Æ├É┬╢├É┬╜├É┬╕. ├É┼╕├É┬╛ default ├æ┬ü├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬▓├É┬╜├æ┬Å├É┬▓├É┬░ ├æ┬ü├æΓé¼├É┬╡├æΓÇ░├æ╞Æ origin/develop.\n$selection = powershell.exe -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File C:/Users/kaloyan.georgiev/Projects/ralph/select-e2e.ps1\n\n# 2. ├É┬á├É┬╡├É┬░├É┬│├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╣ ├æ┬ü├É┬┐├É┬╛├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬┤ output-├É┬░:\nif ($selection -eq 'FULL') {\n    # Cross-cutting ├É┬┐├æ┼á├æΓÇÜ ├É┬╡ bumped (auth, routing, store, locales, shared/components, ├óΓé¼┬ª).\n    # Path-mapping ├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├É┬╝├É┬╛├É┬╢├É┬╡ ├É┬┤├É┬░ ├æΓÇÜ├æΓé¼├É┬╡├É┬╜├æ┬ü├É┬╡ global state side-effects ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ ├É┬┐├æ┼á├É┬╗├É┬╡├É┬╜ suite.\n    npm run e2e\n}\nelseif ($selection) {\n    # 1+ feature ├É┬╝├É┬░p├É┬╜├É┬░├æΓÇÜ ├óΓÇáΓÇÖ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬░├æΓé¼├É┬│├É┬╡├æΓÇÜ├É┬╕├æΓé¼├É┬░├É┬╜ run ├É┬╜├É┬░ ├É┬║├É┬╛├É┬╜├É┬║├æΓé¼├É┬╡├æΓÇÜ├É┬╜├É┬╕ spec ├æΓÇ₧├É┬░├É┬╣├É┬╗├É┬╛├É┬▓├É┬╡/├É┬┐├É┬░├É┬┐├É┬║├É┬╕.\n    npm run e2e -- $selection.Split()\n}\nelse {\n    # Diff ├É┬╜├É┬╡ ├æ┬ü├æ┼á├É┬┤├æ┼á├æΓé¼├É┬╢├É┬░ UI-relevant ├É┬┐├æΓé¼├É┬╛├É┬╝├É┬╡├É┬╜├É┬╕ (backend-only, docs, ralph/, ├æΓÇí├É┬╕├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ├É┬╛ ├æΓÇÜ├É┬╡├æ┬ü├æΓÇÜ ├æΓÇ₧├É┬░├É┬╣├É┬╗├É┬╛├É┬▓├É┬╡).\n    # Skip E2E. the Reports form takes it directly.\n *\n * Skips gracefully when the stack/seed/catalog is unavailable so the suite stays\n * green on environments without the COMP-04 deployment, while genuinely exercising\n * the download when they are present.\n */\nconst SEED_COMPOSITION_ID = 40004;\nconst SEED_TRAIN_NUMBER = '╨æ╨Æ 1612';\nconst SEED_DATE = '2026-06-18';\n\n// ZIP local-file-header magic ΓÇö every .xlsx (OOXML) begins with it.\nconst ZIP_MAGIC = Buffer.from([0x50, 0x4b, 0x03, 0x04]);\n\nasync function readDownloadBuffer(download: Download): Promise<Buffer> {\n  const stream = await download.createReadStream();\n  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];\n  for await (const chunk of stream) {\n    chunks.push(Buffer.isBuffer(chunk) ? chunk : Buffer.from(chunk));\n  }\n  return Buffer.concat(chunks);\n}\n\n/**\n * Minimal central-directory ZIP reader ΓÇö extracts one named entry from an\n * .xlsx (OOXML) buffer without pulling in a third-party dependency. Walks the\n * End-Of-Central-Directory record so it tolerates data descriptors, then\n * inflates STORE (0) or DEFLATE (8) members.\n */\nfunction extractZipEntry(zip: Buffer, entryName: string): string | null {\n  const EOCD_SIG = 0x06054b50;\n  let eocd = -1;\n  for (let i = zip.length - 22; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (zip.readUInt32LE(i) === EOCD_SIG) {\n      eocd = i;\n      break;\n    }\n  }\n  if (eocd < 0) return null;\n\n  const entryCount = zip.readUInt16LE(eocd + 10);\n  let p = zip.readUInt32LE(eocd + 16); // central directory offset\n\n  for (let n = 0; n < entryCount; n++) {\n    if (zip.readUInt32LE(p) !== 0x02014b50) break;\n    const method = zip.readUInt16LE(p + 10);\n    const compSize = zip.readUInt32LE(p + 20);\n    const nameLen = zip.readUInt16LE(p + 28);\n    const extraLen = zip.readUInt16LE(p + 30);\n    const commentLen = zip.readUInt16LE(p + 32);\n    const localOffset = zip.readUInt32LE(p + 42);\n    const name = zip.toString('utf8', p + 46, p + 46 + nameLen);\n\n    if (name === entryName) {\n      const lhNameLen = zip.readUInt16LE(localOffset + 26);\n      const lhExtraLen = zip.readUInt16LE(localOffset + 28);\n      const dataStart = localOffset + 30 + lhNameLen + lhExtraLen;\n      const data = zip.subarray(dataStart, dataStart + compSize);\n      const out = method === 0 ? data : inflateRawSync(data);\n      return out.toString('utf8');\n    }\n    p += 46 + nameLen + extraLen + commentLen;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** All worksheet shared-string text concatenated (free + occupied cells alike). */\nfunction sharedStrings(zip: Buffer): string {\n  return extractZipEntry(zip, 'xl/sharedStrings.xml') ?? '';\n}\n\n/** Ordered shared-string table ΓÇö index N resolves a cell's `<v>N</v>` to its text. */\nfunction sharedStringList(zip: Buffer): string[] {\n  const xml = sharedStrings(zip);\n  return [...xml.matchAll(/<(?:x:)?si>(.*?)<\\/(?:x:)?si>/gs)].map((si) =>\n    [...si[1].matchAll(/<(?:x:)?t[^>]*>(.*?)<\\/(?:x:)?t>/gs)].map((t) => t[1]).join(''),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction worksheetXml(zip: Buffer, index: number): string | null {\n  return extractZipEntry(zip, `xl/worksheets/sheet${index}.xml`);\n}\n\nasync function assertXlsxDownload(download: Download): Promise<Buffer> {\n  expect(download.suggestedFilename()).toMatch(/\\.xlsx$/i);\n  const buffer = await readDownloadBuffer(download);\n  expect(buffer.subarray(0, 4).equals(ZIP_MAGIC)).toBe(true);\n  return buffer;\n}\n\n/**\n * Regression guard for the COMP-04 occupancy bug: the report used to emit only\n * SOLD/BLOCKED seats, so every wagon read 100.00 %. With the full seat├ùsegment\n * grid, free seats appear as empty cells and occupancy must be strictly < 100.\n */\nfunction assertOccupancyBelowFull(zip: Buffer) {\n  const strings = sharedStrings(zip);\n  const matches = [...strings.matchAll(/╨ù╨░╨╡╤é╨╛╤ü╤é ╨╜╨░ ╨▓╨░╨│╨╛╨╜╨░:\\s*([\\d.]+)\\s*%/g)];\n  expect(matches.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  for (const m of matches) {\n    const percent = Number.parseFloat(m[1]);\n    expect(percent).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(percent).toBeLessThan(100);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve the indices in the shared-string table for a given set of cell texts.\n */\nfunction stringIndices(zip: Buffer, ...texts: string[]): Set<number> {\n  const wanted = new Set(texts);\n  const idx = new Set<number>();\n  sharedStringList(zip).forEach((s, i) => {\n    if (wanted.has(s)) idx.add(i);\n  });\n  return idx;\n}\n\n/** Count body (non-column-A) shared-string cells across all wagon sheets whose text is one of `idx`. */\nfunction countBodyCells(zip: Buffer, idx: Set<number>): number {\n  let count = 0;\n  for (let sheet = 1; ; sheet++) {\n    const xml = worksheetXml(zip, sheet);\n    if (xml === null) break;\n    for (const rowMatch of xml.matchAll(/<(?:x:)?row[^>]*>(.*?)<\\/(?:x:)?row>/gs)) {\n      const cells = [\n        ...rowMatch[1].matchAll(\n          /<(?:x:)?c r=\"([A-Z]+)\\d+\"([^>]*)>(?:<(?:x:)?v>(\\d+)<\\/(?:x:)?v>)?<\\/(?:x:)?c>/g,\n        ),\n      ];\n      for (const c of cells) {\n        if (c[1] === 'A') continue;\n        if (/t=\"s\"/.test(c[2]) && c[3] !== undefined && idx.has(Number(c[3]))) count++;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\n/**\n * Regression guard: the COMP-04 bug rendered EVERY matrix cell empty (only the\n * header % was non-zero). The full grid must carry real occupancy marks ΓÇö both\n * occupied (Γ£ô) and blocked (╨æ) ΓÇö in the body of the matrix.\n */\nfunction assertOccupiedAndBlockedPopulated(zip: Buffer) {\n  expect(countBodyCells(zip, stringIndices(zip, 'Γ£ô'))).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n  expect(countBodyCells(zip, stringIndices(zip, '╨æ'))).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n}\n\n/** Free seats must render as 'X' (not empty) so the table never looks broken. */\nfunction assertFreeCellsShowX(zip: Buffer) {\n  expect(countBodyCells(zip, stringIndices(zip, 'X'))).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n}\n\n/**\n * The matrix must have visible grid lines: ClosedXML serialises the thin border\n * we apply to the matrix range as a `style=\"thin\"` border definition in\n * xl/styles.xml.\n */\nfunction assertMatrixHasThinBorders(zip: Buffer) {\n  const styles = extractZipEntry(zip, 'xl/styles.xml') ??
